# What ChatGPT sees: stored titles vs live pages (DLC-77)

Generated by `growth/evals/storedcopy.py` at 2026-10-29T09:50:27Z. Do not edit by hand; rerun it.

One row per chat in which a dealectric.com page showed up in ChatGPT's search results (`#rank`) or as a source pill (`pill`). **Version** is the deployed version whose `<title>` the stored title matches (v1 = the page's first deploy; the version list is under each table). **Reflects** is the cart date that version carried. **Behind** is how many days old the stored copy was when ChatGPT showed it, as a range: since the matching version was replaced, and since it first went live (0 = the live version). **Quoted** is the cart date the answer itself quoted ("chat day" = the date of the chat, usually ChatGPT's own "as of today", since no index copy is that new). **OAI / Bing** say whether that crawler's last fetch before the chat saw a version with the stored title, and when it was.

Snippets: no run before 2026-10-28 20:30Z saved them (ui.py kept only domain, title, url, pub_date). ui.py now keeps `snippet`; the column fills from the next reading.

## Which crawl feeds the stored copy

Only chats that showed a superseded version tell the crawlers apart. For those, did the crawler's last fetch before the chat see the version ChatGPT showed?

| OAI-SearchBot | 378 | 1 | 0 |
| OAI-SearchBot, verified rows only | 378 | 1 | 0 |
| bingbot | 1 | 129 | 249 |

## Unverified OpenAI fetches

Scanners send the OAI-SearchBot UA, so a row counts only when Cloudflare verified it or it sits within 10 min of a verified fetch by the same bot (one crawl sweep: from 2026-10-28 15:29Z Cloudflare leaves part of OpenAI's genuine crawl unverified). Times marked `*` below are such sweep rows. OAI-SearchBot 200 rows: 191 verified, 13 unverified in a verified sweep, 3 unverified and dropped.

Pages fetched in a sweep without a verified row: `/`, `/aurzen-discount-codes`, `/baseus-discount-codes`, `/discount-codes`, `/img/marshall-emberton-iii.png`, `/lifepo4-vs-nmc-power-stations`, `/marshall-emberton-iii`, `/portable-power-stations`, `/products`, `/solar-generator-with-panels-included`, `/soundcore-discount-codes`.

Sweep rows that a later chat's title confirms (verified rows alone would put an older version in the index): `/` 10-29 06:24, `/discount-codes` 10-29 06:38, `/aurzen-discount-codes` 10-29 06:38.

Dropped fetches that a later chat's title confirms (the only fetch that saw the version shown): none.
